# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
# config.py
#
# ׳׳˜׳¨׳”: ׳§׳¨׳™׳׳× ׳›׳ ׳”׳’׳“׳¨׳•׳× ׳”׳¡׳‘׳™׳‘׳” ׳©׳׳©׳×׳ ׳™ ׳¡׳‘׳™׳‘׳” (environment variables).
#   Docker ׳׳¢׳‘׳™׳¨ ׳׳× ׳”׳¢׳¨׳›׳™׳ ׳“׳¨׳ docker-compose.yml.
#   Pydantic-Settings ׳׳˜׳₪׳ ׳‘׳§׳¨׳™׳׳”, ׳ ׳™׳×׳•׳—, ׳•׳‘׳¨׳™׳¨׳•׳× ׳׳—׳“׳.
#   instance ׳™׳—׳™׳“ "settings" ׳׳©׳•׳×׳£ ׳׳›׳ ׳”׳§׳•׳“ ג€” Singleton pattern.
# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€

from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings


class Settings(BaseSettings):

    # --- ׳׳¦׳׳׳” ---
    # ׳׳™׳ ׳“׳§׳¡ ׳”׳׳¦׳׳׳” (0 = ׳׳¦׳׳׳” ׳¨׳׳©׳™׳× ׳©׳ ׳”׳׳—׳©׳‘)
    camera_index:  int = 0
    frame_width:   int = 640
    frame_height:  int = 480

    # --- ׳©׳¨׳× ---
    # ׳”׳₪׳•׳¨׳˜ ׳©׳”׳©׳™׳¨׳•׳× ׳׳׳–׳™׳ ׳¢׳׳™׳•
    port: int = 8010

    # --- Kalman Filter ---
    # process_noise: ׳›׳׳” ׳׳ ׳—׳ ׳• ׳׳׳׳™׳ ׳™׳ ׳©׳”׳׳‘׳˜ ׳–׳–
    #   ג†‘ ׳’׳‘׳•׳” = ׳₪׳—׳•׳× ׳—׳׳§, ׳׳’׳™׳‘ ׳׳”׳¨ ׳™׳•׳×׳¨
    #   ג†“ ׳ ׳׳•׳ = ׳™׳•׳×׳¨ ׳—׳׳§, ׳׳’׳™׳‘ ׳׳׳˜ ׳™׳•׳×׳¨
    kalman_process_noise: float = 0.02

    # measure_noise: ׳›׳׳” ׳׳ ׳—׳ ׳• ׳׳׳׳™׳ ׳™׳ ׳׳׳“׳™׳“׳× MediaPipe
    #   ג†‘ ׳’׳‘׳•׳” = ׳™׳•׳×׳¨ ׳—׳׳§ (׳׳¡׳×׳׳ ׳₪׳—׳•׳× ׳¢׳ ׳”׳׳“׳™׳“׳”)
    #   ג†“ ׳ ׳׳•׳ = ׳₪׳—׳•׳× ׳—׳׳§ (׳׳¡׳×׳׳ ׳™׳•׳×׳¨ ׳¢׳ ׳”׳׳“׳™׳“׳”)
    kalman_measure_noise: float = 0.4

    # --- Calibration ---
    # ׳˜׳•׳•׳— ׳ ׳•׳¨׳׳׳™ ׳©׳ ׳”-iris ׳©׳׳›׳¡׳” ׳׳× ׳”׳׳¡׳ (0.0 - 1.0)
    # ׳ ׳™׳×׳ ׳׳›׳•׳•׳ ׳ ׳׳₪׳™ ׳”׳׳¦׳׳׳” ׳•׳”׳׳©׳×׳׳©
    calib_x_min: float = 0.2
    calib_x_max: float = 0.8
    calib_y_min: float = 0.1
    calib_y_max: float = 0.9

    class Config:
        # .env ג€” ׳©׳™׳׳•׳© ׳׳׳₪׳×׳•׳— ׳§׳•׳™
        env_prefix = "EYE_"
        env_file   = ".env"


# instance ׳™׳—׳™׳“ ׳©׳›׳ ׳”׳§׳•׳“ ׳׳©׳×׳© ׳‘׳• ג€” Singleton pattern
settings = Settings()

# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
# gaze_estimator.py
#
# ׳׳˜׳¨׳”: ׳₪׳•׳×׳— ׳׳× ׳”׳׳¦׳׳׳”, ׳׳¨׳™׳¥ MediaPipe Face Mesh ׳‘thread ׳ ׳₪׳¨׳“,
#   ׳׳—׳©׳‘ ׳׳× ׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳× ׳”׳׳‘׳˜ ׳¢׳ ׳”׳׳¡׳, ׳•׳׳—׳׳™׳§ ׳¢׳ Kalman Filter.
#
# thread-safe: get_latest_gaze() ׳‘׳˜׳•׳— ׳׳§׳¨׳•׳ ׳׳›׳ thread.
#
# ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳:
#   1. ׳§׳¨׳™׳׳× frame ׳׳”׳׳¦׳׳׳” (OpenCV)
#   2. MediaPipe ג†’ 478 ׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ׳›׳•׳׳ iris landmarks
#   3. ׳—׳™׳©׳•׳‘ ׳׳™׳§׳•׳ ׳”׳׳™׳©׳•׳ ׳‘׳™׳—׳¡ ׳׳¢׳™׳ ג†’ ׳›׳™׳•׳•׳ ׳׳‘׳˜ ׳׳ ׳•׳¨׳׳ [-1, 1]
#   4. ׳”׳׳¨׳” ׳׳₪׳™׳§׳¡׳׳™׳ ׳¢׳ ׳”׳׳¡׳ ׳“׳¨׳ calibration
#   5. Kalman Filter ג†’ ׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳” ׳™׳¦׳™׳‘׳” ׳•׳׳—׳•׳׳§׳×
# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import threading
import time
from typing import Dict, Optional

import cv2
import mediapipe as mp
import numpy as np

from app.kalman_filter import GazeKalmanFilter
from app.config import settings

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# ג”€ג”€ MediaPipe iris landmarks (׳׳×׳•׳ 478 ׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳•׳×) ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
# ׳׳¨׳›׳– ׳”׳׳™׳©׳•׳ ג€” ׳”׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳”׳—׳©׳•׳‘׳•׳× ׳‘׳™׳•׳×׳¨ ׳¢׳‘׳•׳¨׳ ׳•
LEFT_IRIS_CENTER  = 473   # ׳¢׳™׳ ׳©׳׳׳׳™׳× (׳׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳× ׳׳‘׳˜ ׳”׳׳¦׳׳׳”)
RIGHT_IRIS_CENTER = 468   # ׳¢׳™׳ ׳™׳׳ ׳™׳×

# ׳₪׳™׳ ׳•׳× ׳¢׳™׳ ׳™׳™׳ ג€” ׳׳ ׳¨׳׳•׳ ׳׳™׳§׳•׳ ׳”׳׳™׳©׳•׳ ׳‘׳×׳•׳ ׳”׳¢׳™׳
LEFT_EYE_INNER  = 133
LEFT_EYE_OUTER  = 33
RIGHT_EYE_INNER = 362
RIGHT_EYE_OUTER = 263

# ׳¢׳₪׳¢׳₪׳™׳™׳ ג€” ׳׳—׳™׳©׳•׳‘ ׳›׳׳” ׳”׳¢׳™׳ ׳₪׳×׳•׳—׳” (confidence)
LEFT_EYE_TOP     = 159
LEFT_EYE_BOTTOM  = 145
RIGHT_EYE_TOP    = 386
RIGHT_EYE_BOTTOM = 374


class GazeEstimator:
    """
    ׳׳¨׳™׳¥ MediaPipe Face Mesh ׳‘thread ׳ ׳₪׳¨׳“.
    ׳׳¡׳₪׳§ gaze point ׳׳¢׳•׳“׳›׳ ׳-main thread ׳“׳¨׳ get_latest_gaze().
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        camera_index:  int   = 0,
        frame_width:   int   = 640,
        frame_height:  int   = 480,
        process_noise: float = 0.02,
        measure_noise: float = 0.4,
    ) -> None:
        self._camera_index  = camera_index
        self._frame_width   = frame_width
        self._frame_height  = frame_height

        # thread safety
        self._lock   = threading.Lock()
        self._latest: Optional[Dict] = None
        self._running = False
        self._thread: Optional[threading.Thread] = None

        # Kalman Filter ׳׳”׳—׳׳§׳× ׳¨׳¢׳© ׳¢׳™׳ ׳™׳™׳
        self._kalman = GazeKalmanFilter(
            process_noise=process_noise,
            measure_noise=measure_noise,
        )

        # ׳’׳•׳“׳ ׳׳¡׳ ג€” ׳‘׳¨׳™׳¨׳× ׳׳—׳“׳ FullHD
        # ׳ ׳™׳×׳ ׳׳¢׳“׳›׳ ׳׳”׳׳§׳•׳— ׳‘׳¢׳×׳™׳“
        self._screen_w = 1920
        self._screen_h = 1080

    # ג”€ג”€ Public API ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€

    @property
    def is_running(self) -> bool:
        return self._running

    def start(self) -> None:
        """׳׳×׳—׳™׳ ׳׳× thread ׳”׳׳¦׳׳׳”."""
        self._running = True
        self._thread  = threading.Thread(
            target=self._run_loop,
            daemon=True,
            name="GazeEstimator",
        )
        self._thread.start()
        logger.info("GazeEstimator started (camera %d)", self._camera_index)

    def stop(self) -> None:
        """׳¢׳•׳¦׳¨ ׳׳× thread ׳”׳׳¦׳׳׳” ׳•׳׳©׳—׳¨׳¨ ׳׳©׳׳‘׳™׳."""
        self._running = False
        if self._thread:
            self._thread.join(timeout=3.0)
        logger.info("GazeEstimator stopped")

    def get_latest_gaze(self) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """
        ׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ ׳׳× ׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳× ׳”׳׳‘׳˜ ׳”׳׳—׳¨׳•׳ ׳”.
        Thread-safe ג€” ׳‘׳˜׳•׳— ׳׳§׳¨׳•׳ ׳׳”-broadcast loop.

        Returns:
            {"x": float, "y": float, "confidence": float} ׳׳• None ׳׳ ׳׳™׳ ׳₪׳ ׳™׳
        """
        with self._lock:
            return self._latest

    def set_screen_size(self, width: int, height: int) -> None:
        """׳¢׳“׳›׳•׳ ׳’׳•׳“׳ ׳׳¡׳ (׳™׳›׳•׳ ׳׳”׳’׳™׳¢ ׳׳”-Angular ׳‘׳¢׳×׳™׳“)."""
        self._screen_w = width
        self._screen_h = height
        logger.info("Screen size updated: %dx%d", width, height)

    # ג”€ג”€ Main Loop (׳¨׳¥ ׳‘-thread ׳ ׳₪׳¨׳“) ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€

    def _run_loop(self) -> None:
        mp_face_mesh = mp.solutions.face_mesh

        # ׳₪׳×׳™׳—׳× ׳׳¦׳׳׳”
        cap = cv2.VideoCapture(self._camera_index)
        cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH,  self._frame_width)
        cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT, self._frame_height)
        cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS, 30)

        if not cap.isOpened():
            logger.error("Cannot open camera %d", self._camera_index)
            self._running = False
            return

        logger.info("Camera opened successfully")

        # ׳”׳₪׳¢׳׳× MediaPipe Face Mesh
        # refine_landmarks=True ג†’ ׳׳₪׳¢׳™׳ iris landmarks (׳—׳•׳‘׳”!)
        with mp_face_mesh.FaceMesh(
            max_num_faces=1,
            refine_landmarks=True,
            min_detection_confidence=0.5,
            min_tracking_confidence=0.5,
        ) as face_mesh:

            while self._running:
                ret, frame = cap.read()
                if not ret:
                    logger.warning("Frame capture failed ג€” retrying...")
                    time.sleep(0.1)
                    continue

                # MediaPipe ׳“׳•׳¨׳© RGB (OpenCV ׳ ׳•׳×׳ BGR)
                rgb = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
                rgb.flags.writeable = False  # ׳׳•׳₪׳˜׳™׳׳™׳–׳¦׳™׳”

                results = face_mesh.process(rgb)

                if results.multi_face_landmarks:
                    lm    = results.multi_face_landmarks[0].landmark
                    gaze  = self._compute_gaze(lm, frame.shape)

                    if gaze:
                        # Kalman smoothing ג€” ׳׳—׳׳™׳§ ׳׳× ׳”׳¨׳¢׳©
                        sx, sy = self._kalman.update(gaze["raw_x"], gaze["raw_y"])
                        with self._lock:
                            self._latest = {
                                "x":          sx,
                                "y":          sy,
                                "confidence": gaze["confidence"],
                            }
                else:
                    # ׳׳™׳ ׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ׳‘׳₪׳¨׳™׳™׳ ג€” ׳׳׳₪׳¡ Kalman ׳•׳׳ ׳§׳”
                    self._kalman.reset()
                    with self._lock:
                        self._latest = None

        cap.release()
        logger.info("Camera released")

    # ג”€ג”€ Gaze Computation ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€

    def _compute_gaze(
        self,
        lm:          list,
        frame_shape: tuple,
    ) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """
        ׳׳—׳©׳‘ ׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳× ׳׳‘׳˜ ׳-landmarks ׳©׳ MediaPipe.

        ׳©׳׳‘׳™׳:
          1. ׳׳™׳§׳•׳ ׳׳™׳©׳•׳ (׳׳׳•׳¦׳¢ ׳©׳×׳™ ׳¢׳™׳ ׳™׳™׳)
          2. ׳ ׳¨׳׳•׳ ׳‘׳™׳—׳¡ ׳׳¨׳•׳—׳‘ ׳”׳¢׳™׳ ג†’ [-1, 1]
          3. ׳׳׳•׳¦׳¢ ׳©׳×׳™ ׳¢׳™׳ ׳™׳™׳
          4. ׳”׳׳¨׳” ׳׳₪׳™׳§׳¡׳׳™׳ ׳׳¡׳

        Returns:
            {"raw_x": float, "raw_y": float, "confidence": float} ׳׳• None
        """
        h, w = frame_shape[:2]

        def to_px(idx: int) -> np.ndarray:
            return np.array([lm[idx].x * w, lm[idx].y * h])

        # ג”€ג”€ ׳׳™׳§׳•׳ ׳׳™׳©׳•׳ ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
        left_iris  = to_px(LEFT_IRIS_CENTER)
        right_iris = to_px(RIGHT_IRIS_CENTER)

        # ג”€ג”€ ׳₪׳™׳ ׳•׳× ׳¢׳™׳ ׳™׳™׳ ׳׳ ׳¨׳׳•׳ ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
        left_inner  = to_px(LEFT_EYE_INNER)
        left_outer  = to_px(LEFT_EYE_OUTER)
        right_inner = to_px(RIGHT_EYE_INNER)
        right_outer = to_px(RIGHT_EYE_OUTER)

        left_eye_w  = np.linalg.norm(left_inner  - left_outer)
        right_eye_w = np.linalg.norm(right_inner - right_outer)

        if left_eye_w < 1 or right_eye_w < 1:
            return None  # ׳¢׳™׳ ׳׳—׳•׳¥ ׳׳₪׳¨׳™׳™׳

        # ג”€ג”€ iris position ׳׳ ׳•׳¨׳׳ [-1, 1] ׳‘׳×׳•׳ ׳”׳¢׳™׳ ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
        left_center  = (left_inner  + left_outer)  / 2
        right_center = (right_inner + right_outer) / 2

        left_norm  = (left_iris  - left_center)  / (left_eye_w  / 2)
        right_norm = (right_iris - right_center) / (right_eye_w / 2)

        avg_norm = (left_norm + right_norm) / 2

        # ג”€ג”€ confidence = ׳›׳׳” ׳”׳¢׳™׳ ׳₪׳×׳•׳—׳” ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
        left_open  = np.linalg.norm(to_px(LEFT_EYE_TOP)  - to_px(LEFT_EYE_BOTTOM))
        right_open = np.linalg.norm(to_px(RIGHT_EYE_TOP) - to_px(RIGHT_EYE_BOTTOM))
        avg_open   = (left_open + right_open) / 2

        # 12px = ׳¢׳™׳ ׳₪׳×׳•׳—׳” ׳׳’׳׳¨׳™. ׳₪׳—׳•׳× ׳-15% = ׳¢׳™׳ ׳¢׳¦׳•׳׳”
        confidence = float(np.clip(avg_open / 12.0, 0.0, 1.0))
        if confidence < 0.15:
            return None

        # ג”€ג”€ ׳”׳׳¨׳” ׳׳₪׳™׳§׳¡׳׳™׳ ׳׳¡׳ ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
        # ׳”׳₪׳•׳ X ג€” ׳”׳׳¦׳׳׳” ׳”׳™׳ mirror
        norm_x = (-avg_norm[0] * 0.5 + 0.5)  # [0, 1], 0.5 = ׳׳¨׳›׳–
        norm_y = ( avg_norm[1] * 0.5 + 0.5)

        # stretch ׳׳₪׳™ calibration ׳-config
        cx_min, cx_max = settings.calib_x_min, settings.calib_x_max
        cy_min, cy_max = settings.calib_y_min, settings.calib_y_max

        norm_x = (norm_x - cx_min) / (cx_max - cx_min)
        norm_y = (norm_y - cy_min) / (cy_max - cy_min)

        screen_x = float(np.clip(norm_x * self._screen_w, 0, self._screen_w))
        screen_y = float(np.clip(norm_y * self._screen_h, 0, self._screen_h))

        return {
            "raw_x":      screen_x,
            "raw_y":      screen_y,
            "confidence": confidence,
        }

================================================================================
FILE: E:\B\Project\GazeConnect\services\python\eye_tracking\app\kalman_filter.py

# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
# kalman_filter.py
#
# ׳׳˜׳¨׳”: ׳”׳—׳׳§׳× ׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳׳‘׳˜ ׳’׳•׳׳׳™׳•׳× ׳-MediaPipe.
#
# ׳”׳‘׳¢׳™׳”: ׳¢׳™׳ ׳™׳™׳ ׳¨׳•׳¢׳“׳•׳× ׳×׳׳™׳“ (Nystagmus, ׳¨׳¢׳™׳“׳•׳× ׳˜׳‘׳¢׳™׳•׳×, ׳¨׳¢׳© ׳׳¦׳׳׳”).
#   ׳‘׳׳™ ׳₪׳™׳׳˜׳¨: ׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳× ׳”׳׳‘׳˜ ׳§׳•׳₪׳¦׳× ֲ±50px ׳‘׳›׳ frame ג†’ DwellTime ׳׳ ׳¢׳•׳‘׳“.
#   ׳¢׳ ׳₪׳™׳׳˜׳¨:  ׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳” ׳™׳¦׳™׳‘׳” ׳•׳׳“׳•׳™׳§׳× ג†’ DwellTime ׳¢׳•׳‘׳“ ׳׳¦׳•׳™׳.
#
# ׳׳•׳“׳: state = [x, y, vx, vy] ג€” ׳׳™׳§׳•׳ + ׳׳”׳™׳¨׳•׳×
#        obs   = [x, y]         ג€” ׳׳” ׳©MediaPipe ׳׳“׳“
# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np


class GazeKalmanFilter:
    """
    Kalman Filter ׳“׳•-׳׳׳“׳™ ׳׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳× ׳׳‘׳˜.

    ׳₪׳¨׳׳˜׳¨׳™׳:
        process_noise  ג€” ׳›׳׳” ׳׳ ׳—׳ ׳• ׳׳׳׳™׳ ׳™׳ ׳©׳”׳׳‘׳˜ ׳–׳–
                         ג†‘ ׳’׳‘׳•׳” = ׳₪׳—׳•׳× ׳—׳׳§, ׳׳’׳™׳‘ ׳׳”׳¨
                         ג†“ ׳ ׳׳•׳ = ׳™׳•׳×׳¨ ׳—׳׳§, ׳׳’׳™׳‘ ׳׳׳˜
        measure_noise  ג€” ׳›׳׳” ׳׳ ׳—׳ ׳• ׳׳׳׳™׳ ׳™׳ ׳׳׳“׳™׳“׳”
                         ג†‘ ׳’׳‘׳•׳” = ׳™׳•׳×׳¨ ׳—׳׳§ (׳׳×׳¢׳׳ ׳׳”׳׳“׳™׳“׳”)
                         ג†“ ׳ ׳׳•׳ = ׳₪׳—׳•׳× ׳—׳׳§ (׳׳׳׳™׳ ׳׳׳“׳™׳“׳”)
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        process_noise: float = 0.02,
        measure_noise: float = 0.4,
    ) -> None:
        # ג”€ג”€ ׳׳˜׳¨׳™׳¦׳× ׳׳¢׳‘׳¨ (׳§׳™׳ ׳׳˜׳™׳§׳”: x += vx*dt, y += vy*dt, dt=1) ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
        self.F = np.array([
            [1, 0, 1, 0],
            [0, 1, 0, 1],
            [0, 0, 1, 0],
            [0, 0, 0, 1],
        ], dtype=float)

        # ג”€ג”€ ׳׳˜׳¨׳™׳¦׳× ׳׳“׳™׳“׳” (׳¨׳•׳׳™׳ ׳¨׳§ x, y ג€” ׳׳ vx, vy) ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
        self.H = np.array([
            [1, 0, 0, 0],
            [0, 1, 0, 0],
        ], dtype=float)

        # ג”€ג”€ ׳¨׳¢׳© ׳×׳”׳׳™׳ (Q) ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
        q = process_noise
        self.Q = np.diag([q, q, q, q])

        # ג”€ג”€ ׳¨׳¢׳© ׳׳“׳™׳“׳” (R) ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
        r = measure_noise
        self.R = np.diag([r, r])

        # ג”€ג”€ ׳׳¦׳‘ ׳”׳×׳—׳׳×׳™ ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
        self.x = np.zeros((4, 1), dtype=float)    # [x, y, vx, vy]
        self.P = np.eye(4, dtype=float) * 100.0   # ׳׳™-׳•׳•׳“׳׳•׳× ׳’׳‘׳•׳”׳” ׳‘׳”׳×׳—׳׳”
        self._initialized = False

    def update(self, measured_x: float, measured_y: float) -> tuple[float, float]:
        """
        ׳׳§׳‘׳ ׳׳“׳™׳“׳” ׳’׳•׳׳׳™׳× ׳•׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ ׳׳™׳§׳•׳ ׳׳•׳—׳׳§.

        Args:
            measured_x: ׳׳™׳§׳•׳ X ׳׳׳“׳™׳”-׳₪׳™׳™׳₪ (׳₪׳™׳§׳¡׳׳™׳ ׳׳¡׳)
            measured_y: ׳׳™׳§׳•׳ Y ׳׳׳“׳™׳”-׳₪׳™׳™׳₪ (׳₪׳™׳§׳¡׳׳™׳ ׳׳¡׳)

        Returns:
            tuple (smooth_x, smooth_y)
        """
        z = np.array([[measured_x], [measured_y]], dtype=float)

        if not self._initialized:
            self.x = np.array([[measured_x], [measured_y], [0.0], [0.0]])
            self._initialized = True
            return measured_x, measured_y

        # ג”€ג”€ Predict ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
        x_pred = self.F @ self.x
        P_pred = self.F @ self.P @ self.F.T + self.Q

        # ג”€ג”€ Update ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
        y_innov = z - self.H @ x_pred
        S       = self.H @ P_pred @ self.H.T + self.R
        K       = P_pred @ self.H.T @ np.linalg.inv(S)  # Kalman gain

        self.x = x_pred + K @ y_innov
        self.P = (np.eye(4) - K @ self.H) @ P_pred

        return float(self.x[0, 0]), float(self.x[1, 0])

    def reset(self) -> None:
        """׳׳™׳₪׳•׳¡ ׳”׳₪׳™׳׳˜׳¨ ג€” ׳§׳•׳¨׳” ׳›׳©׳׳™׳ ׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ׳‘׳₪׳¨׳™׳™׳."""
        self.x = np.zeros((4, 1), dtype=float)
        self.P = np.eye(4, dtype=float) * 100.0
        self._initialized = False

# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
# main.py
#
# ׳׳˜׳¨׳”: ׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳× ׳›׳ ׳™׳¡׳” ג€” ׳‘׳•׳ ׳” FastAPI app, ׳׳—׳‘׳¨ routes, ׳׳ ׳”׳ lifespan.
#   ׳׳•׳×׳• ׳₪׳˜׳¨׳ ׳›׳׳• face_recognition/app/main.py.
#
#   ׳׳—׳¨׳™ ׳”׳₪׳™׳¦׳•׳ main.py ׳”׳¨׳‘׳” ׳™׳•׳×׳¨ ׳§׳¦׳¨ ׳•׳‘׳¨׳•׳¨:
#   - ConnectionManager ׳•-WebSocket endpoint ג†’ api/ws.py
#   - Pydantic models                        ג†’ core/models.py
#   - GazeEstimator                          ג†’ gaze_estimator.py
#   - KalmanFilter                           ג†’ kalman_filter.py
#   - Settings                               ג†’ config.py
# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€

import asyncio
import json
import logging
import time
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

import uvicorn
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware

from app.config import settings
from app.gaze_estimator import GazeEstimator
from app.api.ws import manager, router as ws_router
from app.core.models import HealthResponse

# ׳”׳’׳“׳¨׳× logging ג€” ׳₪׳•׳¨׳˜ ׳©׳•׳¨׳” ׳–׳¨׳”, ׳•׳§׳¦׳¨ (׳–׳”׳” ׳-face_recognition)
logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s | %(levelname)s | %(name)s | %(message)s"
)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# global ג€” ׳ ׳’׳™׳© ׳-lifespan ׳•׳-health endpoint
gaze_estimator: GazeEstimator | None = None


# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
# Lifespan ג€” startup / shutdown (׳׳•׳×׳• ׳₪׳˜׳¨׳ ׳›׳׳• face_recognition)
# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€

@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    """
    ׳ ׳™׳”׳•׳ ׳—׳–׳•׳¨ ׳”׳—׳™׳™׳ ׳©׳ ׳”׳₪׳׳™׳§׳¦׳™׳”.
    ׳§׳•׳“ ׳׳₪׳ ׳™ yield = startup, ׳§׳•׳“ ׳׳—׳¨׳™ yield = shutdown.
    """
    global gaze_estimator

    # ג”€ג”€ STARTUP ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
    logger.info("Starting GazeConnect Eye Tracking Service...")

    # 1. ׳™׳¦׳™׳¨׳× GazeEstimator (׳₪׳•׳×׳— ׳׳¦׳׳׳” + ׳˜׳•׳¢׳ MediaPipe)
    logger.info("Opening camera (index=%d)...", settings.camera_index)
    gaze_estimator = GazeEstimator(
        camera_index=settings.camera_index,
        frame_width=settings.frame_width,
        frame_height=settings.frame_height,
        process_noise=settings.kalman_process_noise,
        measure_noise=settings.kalman_measure_noise,
    )
    gaze_estimator.start()

    # 2. ׳©׳׳™׳¨׳” ׳‘-app.state (׳–׳׳™׳ ׳׳›׳ endpoint)
    app.state.estimator = gaze_estimator

    # 3. ׳”׳₪׳¢׳׳× broadcast loop ׳‘׳¨׳§׳¢
    task = asyncio.create_task(_broadcast_loop())

    logger.info("ג“ Eye Tracking Service is ready on port %d!", settings.port)

    yield  # ג† ׳”׳©׳™׳¨׳•׳× ׳¨׳¥ ׳•׳§׳‘׳ ׳‘׳§׳©׳•׳×

    # ג”€ג”€ SHUTDOWN ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
    logger.info("Shutting down Eye Tracking Service...")
    task.cancel()
    if gaze_estimator:
        gaze_estimator.stop()
    logger.info("Camera closed. Goodbye!")


async def _broadcast_loop() -> None:
    """
    ׳¨׳¥ ׳‘׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳›׳ 33ms (30fps).
    ׳©׳•׳׳— gaze point ׳׳›׳ Angular clients ׳׳—׳•׳‘׳¨׳™׳.
    """
    while True:
        start = time.monotonic()

        if manager.has_clients and gaze_estimator:
            point = gaze_estimator.get_latest_gaze()
            if point:
                payload = json.dumps({
                    "x":          round(point["x"], 1),
                    "y":          round(point["y"], 1),
                    "confidence": round(point["confidence"], 3),
                    "ts":         int(time.time() * 1000),
                })
                await manager.broadcast(payload)

        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        await asyncio.sleep(max(0.0, 0.033 - elapsed))


# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
# FastAPI App
# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€

app = FastAPI(
    title="GazeConnect Eye Tracking Service",
    description="MediaPipe Face Mesh ג†’ Gaze Point ג†’ WebSocket ג†’ Angular",
    version="1.0.0",
    lifespan=lifespan,
)

# CORS ג€” ׳₪׳©׳¨ ׳Angular (localhost:4200) ׳׳’׳©׳× ׳׳©׳™׳¨׳•׳×
app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=["http://localhost:4200", "http://localhost:5000"],
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

# ג”€ג”€ Routes ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
app.include_router(ws_router)   # WebSocket: /ws/gaze


# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
# Endpoints
# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€

@app.get("/health", response_model=HealthResponse)
async def health():
    """׳‘׳“׳™׳§׳× ׳×׳§׳™׳ ׳•׳× ג€” Docker healthcheck ׳§׳•׳¨׳ ׳׳›׳׳."""
    return HealthResponse(
        status="ok",
        camera_ok=gaze_estimator.is_running if gaze_estimator else False,
        clients=manager.client_count,
    )

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from uuid import UUID
from app.core.models import ContactResponse
from app.infrastructure.postgres_store import PostgresContactStore
from app.infrastructure.redis_cache import RedisEmbeddingCache
from app.api.dependencies import get_contact_store, get_cache
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

router = APIRouter()


@router.get(
    "/contacts",
    response_model=list[ContactResponse],
    summary="Get all known contacts",
    description="Returns all enrolled contacts without their embeddings."
)
async def get_contacts(
    store: PostgresContactStore = Depends(get_contact_store)
) -> list[ContactResponse]:
    """
    ׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ ׳׳× ׳›׳ ׳׳ ׳©׳™ ׳”׳§׳©׳¨ ׳”׳׳•׳›׳¨׳™׳.
    ׳”-Admin Panel ׳׳©׳×׳׳© ׳‘׳–׳” ׳׳”׳¦׳™׳’ ׳׳× ׳”׳¨׳©׳™׳׳”.
    ׳”-AAC Board ׳׳©׳×׳׳© ׳‘׳–׳” ׳׳”׳¦׳™׳’ ׳›׳₪׳×׳•׳¨׳™ ׳׳ ׳©׳™׳.
    """
    contacts = await store.get_all_contacts()

    return [
        ContactResponse(
            id=c.id,
            name=c.name,
            photo_url=c.photo_url,
            enrolled_at=c.enrolled_at
        )
        for c in contacts
    ]


@router.delete(
    "/contacts/{contact_id}",
    status_code=204,
    summary="Delete a contact",
    description="Removes a contact and invalidates the recognition cache."
)
async def delete_contact(
    contact_id: UUID,
    store: PostgresContactStore = Depends(get_contact_store),
    cache: RedisEmbeddingCache = Depends(get_cache)
) -> None:
    """
    ׳׳•׳—׳§ ׳׳™׳© ׳§׳©׳¨ ׳׳₪׳™ ID.
    ׳׳ ׳§׳” ׳׳× ׳”-cache ׳›׳“׳™ ׳©׳”׳–׳™׳”׳•׳™ ׳™׳¢׳•׳“׳›׳ ׳׳™׳“.
    """
    deleted = await store.delete_contact(contact_id)

    if not deleted:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail=f"Contact {contact_id} not found"
        )

    # ׳ ׳™׳§׳•׳™ cache ג€” ׳”׳–׳™׳”׳•׳™ ׳׳ ׳™׳–׳”׳” ׳¢׳•׳“ ׳׳× ׳”׳׳“׳ ׳”׳–׳”
    await cache.invalidate()

    logger.info("Deleted contact id=%s", contact_id)
    # 204 No Content ג€” ׳׳™׳ body ׳‘׳×׳©׳•׳‘׳”

from fastapi import Request
from app.infrastructure.postgres_store import PostgresContactStore
from app.infrastructure.redis_cache import RedisEmbeddingCache
from app.infrastructure.insightface_detector import InsightFaceDetector
from app.services.recognition_service import RecognitionService
from app.services.enrollment_service import EnrollmentService


def get_contact_store(request: Request) -> PostgresContactStore:
    """
    ׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ ׳׳× ׳”-PostgresContactStore ׳׳”-app state.
    ׳ ׳•׳¦׳¨ ׳₪׳¢׳ ׳׳—׳× ׳‘-startup ׳•׳׳©׳•׳×׳£ ׳׳›׳ ׳”׳‘׳§׳©׳•׳×.
    """
    return request.app.state.contact_store


def get_cache(request: Request) -> RedisEmbeddingCache:
    """
    ׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ ׳׳× ׳”-RedisEmbeddingCache ׳׳”-app state.
    """
    return request.app.state.cache


def get_detector(request: Request) -> InsightFaceDetector:
    """
    ׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ ׳׳× ׳”-InsightFaceDetector ׳׳”-app state.
    ׳”׳׳•׳“׳ ׳ ׳˜׳¢׳ ׳₪׳¢׳ ׳׳—׳× ׳‘-startup (׳›׳‘׳“! ~200MB).
    """
    return request.app.state.detector


def get_recognition_service(request: Request) -> RecognitionService:
    """
    ׳‘׳•׳ ׳” RecognitionService ׳׳”-components ׳‘-app state.
    FastAPI ׳™׳¦׳•׳¨ instance ׳—׳“׳© ׳׳›׳ ׳‘׳§׳©׳” ג€” ׳׳‘׳ ׳”-components ׳¢׳¦׳׳
    (detector, store, cache) ׳׳©׳•׳×׳₪׳™׳.
    """
    return RecognitionService(
        detector=request.app.state.detector,
        store=request.app.state.contact_store,
        cache=request.app.state.cache
    )


def get_enrollment_service(request: Request) -> EnrollmentService:
    """
    ׳‘׳•׳ ׳” EnrollmentService ׳׳”-components ׳‘-app state.
    """
    return EnrollmentService(
        detector=request.app.state.detector,
        store=request.app.state.contact_store,
        cache=request.app.state.cache
    )

# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
# api/enroll.py

# ׳׳˜׳¨׳”: endpoint POST /enroll
# ׳”-Admin ׳׳•׳¡׳™׳£ ׳׳“׳ ׳—׳“׳© (׳׳׳, ׳׳¨׳™׳ ׳”׳¡׳™׳™׳¢׳× ׳•׳›׳•').

# ׳§׳׳˜: multipart/form-data ׳¢׳:
#   name = ׳©׳ ׳”׳׳“׳ ("׳׳׳", "׳׳¨׳™׳")
#   file = ׳×׳׳•׳ ׳” ׳¢׳ ׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ׳‘׳¨׳•׳¨׳•׳×

#
# ׳׳” ׳§׳•׳¨׳”:
#   1. InsightFace ׳׳•׳¦׳ ׳₪׳ ׳™׳
#   2. ׳—׳™׳×׳•׳ + ׳©׳׳™׳¨׳× ׳×׳׳•׳ ׳” ׳׳“׳™׳¡׳§
#   3. ׳©׳׳™׳¨׳× embedding + metadata ׳-DB

# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€

from fastapi import APIRouter, UploadFile, File, Form, Depends, HTTPException
from app.core.models import EnrollResponse
from app.services.enrollment_service import EnrollmentService
from app.api.dependencies import get_enrollment_service
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

router = APIRouter()


@router.post(
    "/enroll",
    response_model=EnrollResponse,
    status_code=201,
    summary="Enroll a new contact",
    description="Register a new person with their name and photo. Returns contact ID and photo URL."
)
async def enroll_contact(
    name: str = Form(..., description="Display name, e.g. '׳׳׳' or '׳׳¨׳™׳ ׳”׳¡׳™׳™׳¢׳×'"),
    file: UploadFile = File(..., description="Clear photo of the person's face"),
    service: EnrollmentService = Depends(get_enrollment_service)
) -> EnrollResponse:
    """
    ׳׳•׳¡׳™׳£ ׳׳“׳ ׳—׳“׳© ׳׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳”׳–׳™׳”׳•׳™.
    ׳”-Admin Panel ׳§׳•׳¨׳ ׳׳–׳” ׳›׳©׳׳•׳¡׳™׳₪׳™׳ ׳׳™׳© ׳§׳©׳¨.
    """
    if not name or not name.strip():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Name cannot be empty")

    image_bytes = await file.read()
    if not image_bytes:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Empty image file")

    logger.info("Enrolling new contact: '%s'", name.strip())

    try:
        result = await service.enroll(name=name.strip(), image_bytes=image_bytes)
        return result

    except ValueError as e:
        # ׳©׳’׳™׳׳•׳× ׳™׳“׳•׳¢׳•׳×: ׳׳ ׳ ׳׳¦׳׳• ׳₪׳ ׳™׳, ׳™׳•׳×׳¨ ׳׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ׳׳—׳“ ׳•׳›׳•'
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=str(e))

# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€

from fastapi import APIRouter, UploadFile, File, Form, Depends, HTTPException
from app.core.models import FaceDetectionResponse
from app.services.recognition_service import RecognitionService
from app.api.dependencies import get_recognition_service
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

router = APIRouter()


@router.post(
    "/recognize",
    response_model=list[FaceDetectionResponse],
    summary="Recognize faces in a camera frame",
    description="Receives a JPEG frame and returns all detected faces with recognition results."
)
async def recognize_faces(
    file: UploadFile = File(..., description="JPEG image from camera"),
    timestamp_ms: str = Form(..., description="Frame capture timestamp (Unix ms)"),
    service: RecognitionService = Depends(get_recognition_service)
) -> list[FaceDetectionResponse]:
    """
    ׳׳§׳‘׳ frame ׳׳”׳׳¦׳׳׳” ג†’ ׳׳–׳”׳” ׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ג†’ ׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ ׳׳™ ׳‘׳×׳׳•׳ ׳”.

    C# ׳§׳•׳¨׳ ׳׳–׳” ׳“׳¨׳ HttpFaceRecognitionClient.RecognizeAsync()
    """
    # ׳§׳¨׳™׳׳× bytes ׳©׳ ׳”׳×׳׳•׳ ׳”
    image_bytes = await file.read()

    if not image_bytes:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Empty image file")

    logger.debug("Processing frame, timestamp_ms=%s, size=%d bytes", timestamp_ms, len(image_bytes))

    # ׳”׳–׳™׳”׳•׳™ ׳¢׳¦׳׳• ג€” ׳”׳׳•׳’׳™׳§׳” ׳‘-RecognitionService
    results = await service.recognize(image_bytes)

    logger.debug("Recognition complete: %d face(s) found", len(results))
    return results

    insightface_model: str = "buffalo_sc"

    photos_dir: str = "/app/photos"

        # ׳׳ ׳§׳™׳™׳ ׳§׳•׳‘׳¥ .env ג€” ׳§׳¨׳ ׳׳•׳×׳• (׳©׳™׳׳•׳©׳™ ׳׳₪׳™׳×׳•׳— ׳׳§׳•׳׳™)

    """
    ׳׳™׳© ׳§׳©׳¨ ׳׳•׳›׳¨ ׳‘׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳×.
    ׳ ׳©׳׳¨ ׳‘׳˜׳‘׳׳× contacts ׳‘-PostgreSQL.

    name: str                         # ׳©׳ ׳׳×׳¦׳•׳’׳” ׳¢׳ ׳”׳›׳₪׳×׳•׳¨: "׳׳׳", "׳׳¨׳™׳"
    photo_url: Optional[str] = None   # ׳ ׳×׳™׳‘ ׳׳×׳׳•׳ ׳”: "/photos/uuid.jpg"
    enrolled_at: datetime             # ׳׳×׳™ ׳ ׳¨׳©׳


# ג”€ג”€ BoundingBox ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
class BoundingBox(BaseModel):
    """
    ׳׳™׳§׳•׳ ׳”׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ׳‘׳×׳׳•׳ ׳” ג€” ׳—׳™׳™׳‘ ׳׳”׳×׳׳™׳ ׳‘׳“׳™׳•׳§ ׳-C# BoundingBox record.
    C# ׳׳¦׳₪׳” ׳׳©׳“׳•׳×: x, y, width, height (׳׳•׳×׳™׳•׳× ׳§׳˜׳ ׳•׳× ג€” JSON serialization).
    """
    x: int       # ׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳× ׳”׳”׳×׳—׳׳” ג€” ׳¦׳™׳¨ ׳׳•׳₪׳§׳™
    y: int       # ׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳× ׳”׳”׳×׳—׳׳” ג€” ׳¦׳™׳¨ ׳׳ ׳›׳™
    width: int   # ׳¨׳•׳—׳‘ ׳”׳׳׳‘׳
    height: int  # ׳’׳•׳‘׳” ׳”׳׳׳‘׳


# ג”€ג”€ FaceDetectionResponse ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
class FaceDetectionResponse(BaseModel):
    """
    ׳×׳©׳•׳‘׳” ׳׳—׳× ׳©׳ ׳–׳™׳”׳•׳™ ׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ג€” ׳‘׳“׳™׳•׳§ ׳׳” ׳©-C# ׳׳¦׳₪׳” ׳׳§׳‘׳.

    ׳—׳©׳•׳‘ ׳׳׳•׳“: ׳©׳׳•׳× ׳”׳©׳“׳•׳× ׳›׳׳ ׳—׳™׳™׳‘׳™׳ ׳׳”׳×׳׳™׳ ׳-FaceDetectionDto ׳‘-C#:
      person_id   ג†’ PersonId
      person_name ג†’ PersonName
      confidence  ג†’ Confidence
      bounding_box ג†’ BoundingBox
      is_known    ג†’ IsKnown

    FastAPI ׳׳׳™׳¨ ׳׳•׳˜׳•׳׳˜׳™׳× snake_case ג†’ camelCase ׳‘-JSON.
    """
    person_id: Optional[UUID] = None       # null ׳׳ ׳׳ ׳׳•׳›׳¨
    person_name: Optional[str] = None     # null ׳׳ ׳׳ ׳׳•׳›׳¨
    confidence: float                      # 0.0 ׳¢׳“ 1.0
    bounding_box: BoundingBox             # ׳׳™׳§׳•׳ ׳”׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ׳‘׳×׳׳•׳ ׳”
    is_known: bool                         # ׳”׳׳ ׳׳¢׳ ׳¡׳£ ׳”׳–׳™׳”׳•׳™


# ג”€ג”€ EnrollRequest (metadata) ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
class EnrollResponse(BaseModel):

    """
    contact_id: UUID    # ׳”-ID ׳”׳—׳“׳© ׳©׳ ׳•׳¦׳¨

# ג”€ג”€ ContactResponse ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€

    ׳׳ ׳›׳•׳׳ ׳׳× ׳”-embedding (׳•׳§׳˜׳•׳¨ 512 ׳׳¡׳₪׳¨׳™׳) ׳›׳™ ׳”׳•׳ ׳’׳“׳•׳ ׳•׳׳ ׳ ׳—׳•׳¥ ׳-UI.
    """
    id: UUID
    name: str
    photo_url: Optional[str] = None
    enrolled_at: datetime

# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
# infrastructure/insightface_detector.py
#
# ׳׳˜׳¨׳”: ׳׳™׳׳•׳© IFaceDetector ׳¢׳ InsightFace + ArcFace model.
# ׳–׳” ׳”׳׳‘ ׳©׳ ׳”׳©׳™׳¨׳•׳× ג€” ׳”׳§׳•׳“ ׳©׳‘׳׳׳× "׳¨׳•׳׳”" ׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ׳‘׳×׳׳•׳ ׳”.
#
# ׳׳™׳ ׳–׳” ׳¢׳•׳‘׳“:
#   1. ׳׳§׳‘׳ bytes ׳©׳ ׳×׳׳•׳ ׳”
#   2. ׳׳׳™׳¨ ׳-numpy array ׳©OpenCV ׳™׳›׳•׳ ׳׳¢׳‘׳•׳“ ׳׳™׳×׳•
#   3. InsightFace ׳׳•׳¦׳ ׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ׳•׳׳—׳©׳‘ embedding (512 ׳׳¡׳₪׳¨׳™׳)
#   4. ׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ ׳׳× ׳”-embedding + ׳׳™׳§׳•׳ ׳”׳₪׳ ׳™׳
#
# ׳—׳©׳•׳‘: ׳”׳׳•׳“׳ ׳ ׳˜׳¢׳ ׳₪׳¢׳ ׳׳—׳× ׳‘׳”׳₪׳¢׳׳” (׳›׳‘׳“! ~200MB)
# ׳•׳׳ ׳‘׳›׳ ׳‘׳§׳©׳” ג€” ׳–׳• ׳¡׳™׳‘׳” ׳-Singleton pattern.
# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€

import cv2
import numpy as np
import insightface
from insightface.app import FaceAnalysis
from app.core.interfaces import IFaceDetector
from app.config import settings
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class InsightFaceDetector(IFaceDetector):
    """
    ׳–׳™׳”׳•׳™ ׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ׳¢׳ InsightFace ArcFace model.
    ׳”׳׳•׳“׳ buffalo_sc = ׳§׳˜׳ ׳•׳׳”׳™׳¨, ׳׳×׳׳™׳ ׳-CPU.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        # ׳˜׳¢׳™׳ ׳× ׳”׳׳•׳“׳ ג€” ׳§׳•׳¨׳” ׳₪׳¢׳ ׳׳—׳× ׳‘׳”׳₪׳¢׳׳× ׳”׳©׳™׳¨׳•׳×
        # ctx_id=-1 = CPU mode (׳׳™׳ GPU)
        logger.info("Loading InsightFace model '%s'...", settings.insightface_model)
        self._app = FaceAnalysis(name=settings.insightface_model)
        self._app.prepare(ctx_id=-1)
        logger.info("InsightFace model loaded successfully.")

    async def detect_and_embed(
        self,
        image_bytes: bytes
    ) -> list[tuple[np.ndarray, tuple[int, int, int, int]]]:
        """
        ׳׳§׳‘׳ bytes ׳©׳ ׳×׳׳•׳ ׳”, ׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ ׳¨׳©׳™׳׳” ׳©׳ (embedding, bounding_box).

        embedding  = np.ndarray ׳‘׳’׳•׳“׳ (512,) ג€” ׳™׳™׳¦׳•׳’ ׳׳¡׳₪׳¨׳™ ׳©׳ ׳”׳₪׳ ׳™׳
        bounding_box = (x, y, width, height) ג€” ׳׳™׳§׳•׳ ׳‘׳×׳׳•׳ ׳”
        """
        # --- ׳”׳׳¨׳× bytes ג†’ numpy array ׳©OpenCV ׳׳‘׳™׳ ---
        # np.frombuffer: ׳׳׳™׳¨ bytes ׳-array ׳©׳ bytes ׳’׳•׳׳׳™׳™׳
        # cv2.imdecode: ׳׳₪׳¢׳ ׳— JPEG/PNG ׳-BGR image array
        nparr = np.frombuffer(image_bytes, np.uint8)
        img = cv2.imdecode(nparr, cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)

        if img is None:
            logger.warning("Failed to decode image bytes")
            return []

        # --- ׳–׳™׳”׳•׳™ ׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ---
        # get() ׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ ׳¨׳©׳™׳׳× Face objects, ׳›׳ ׳׳—׳“ ׳¢׳:
        #   face.embedding = np.ndarray(512) ג€” ׳”-ArcFace embedding
        #   face.bbox = [x1, y1, x2, y2] ג€” ׳₪׳™׳ ׳•׳× ׳”׳₪׳ ׳™׳
        faces = self._app.get(img)

        if not faces:
            return []

        results = []
        for face in faces:
            embedding = face.embedding  # np.ndarray(512,)

            # ׳”׳׳¨׳× bbox ׳׳₪׳•׳¨׳׳˜ InsightFace [x1,y1,x2,y2]
            # ׳׳₪׳•׳¨׳׳˜ ׳©׳׳ ׳• (x, y, width, height)
            x1, y1, x2, y2 = face.bbox.astype(int)
            bbox = (x1, y1, x2 - x1, y2 - y1)  # (x, y, width, height)

            results.append((embedding, bbox))

        logger.debug("Detected %d face(s) in image", len(results))
        return results

# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
# infrastructure/postgres_store.py
#
# ׳׳˜׳¨׳”: ׳׳™׳׳•׳© IContactStore ג€” ׳©׳׳™׳¨׳” ׳•׳—׳™׳₪׳•׳© ׳׳ ׳©׳™ ׳§׳©׳¨ ׳‘-PostgreSQL.
#
# ׳”׳˜׳‘׳׳” contacts ׳ ׳•׳¦׳¨׳× ׳׳•׳˜׳•׳׳˜׳™׳× ׳‘׳”׳₪׳¢׳׳” ׳”׳¨׳׳©׳•׳ ׳” (CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS).
# ׳׳™׳ ׳¦׳•׳¨׳ ׳‘-migrations ׳™׳“׳ ׳™׳™׳!
#
# ׳˜׳›׳ ׳•׳׳•׳’׳™׳•׳×:
#   asyncpg  = ׳—׳™׳‘׳•׳¨ async ׳׳”׳™׳¨ ׳-PostgreSQL
#   pgvector = ׳×׳׳™׳›׳” ׳‘׳¡׳•׳’ ׳¢׳׳•׳“׳” vector(512) ׳׳—׳™׳©׳•׳‘ ׳“׳׳™׳•׳
#
# ׳—׳™׳©׳•׳‘ ׳“׳׳™׳•׳:
#   cosine similarity = ׳׳“׳“ ׳“׳׳™׳•׳ ׳‘׳™׳ ׳©׳ ׳™ ׳•׳§׳˜׳•׳¨׳™׳ (0 ׳¢׳“ 1)
#   ׳׳—׳•׳©׳‘ ׳™׳©׳™׳¨׳•׳× ׳‘-SQL ׳¢׳ operator <=> ׳©׳ pgvector
#   ׳‘׳™׳¦׳•׳¢׳™׳: O(n) ׳¢׳ CPU ג€” ׳׳¡׳₪׳™׳§ ׳׳¢׳©׳¨׳•׳× ׳׳ ׳©׳™ ׳§׳©׳¨
# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€

import asyncpg
import numpy as np
from uuid import UUID, uuid4
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Optional
import logging

from app.core.interfaces import IContactStore
from app.core.models import Contact
from app.config import settings

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class PostgresContactStore(IContactStore):
    """
    ׳©׳׳™׳¨׳” ׳•׳—׳™׳₪׳•׳© ׳©׳ ׳׳ ׳©׳™ ׳§׳©׳¨ ׳‘-PostgreSQL ׳¢׳ pgvector.
    """

    def __init__(self, pool: asyncpg.Pool):
        # pool = ׳׳׳’׳¨ ׳—׳™׳‘׳•׳¨׳™׳ ג€” ׳›׳ ׳‘׳§׳©׳” ׳׳•׳§׳—׳× ׳—׳™׳‘׳•׳¨ ׳₪׳ ׳•׳™ ׳׳”׳׳׳’׳¨
        # ׳•׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨׳” ׳׳•׳×׳• ׳‘׳¡׳™׳•׳ (׳™׳¢׳™׳ ׳™׳•׳×׳¨ ׳׳—׳™׳‘׳•׳¨ ׳—׳“׳© ׳‘׳›׳ ׳‘׳§׳©׳”)
        self._pool = pool

    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """
        ׳™׳•׳¦׳¨ ׳׳× ׳˜׳‘׳׳× contacts ׳׳ ׳׳ ׳§׳™׳™׳׳×.
        ׳ ׳§׳¨׳ ׳₪׳¢׳ ׳׳—׳× ׳‘׳”׳₪׳¢׳׳× ׳”׳©׳™׳¨׳•׳×.
        """
        async with self._pool.acquire() as conn:
            # ׳”׳₪׳¢׳׳× pgvector extension ג€” ׳—׳•׳‘׳” ׳׳₪׳ ׳™ ׳©׳™׳׳•׳© ׳‘׳¡׳•׳’ vector
            await conn.execute("CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS vector")

            # ׳™׳¦׳™׳¨׳× ׳”׳˜׳‘׳׳” ג€” IF NOT EXISTS = ׳׳ ׳™׳™׳›׳©׳ ׳׳ ׳›׳‘׳¨ ׳§׳™׳™׳
            await conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS contacts (
                    id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
                    name        TEXT NOT NULL,
                    embedding   vector(512) NOT NULL,
                    photo_url   TEXT,
                    enrolled_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
                )
            """)

            # ׳׳™׳ ׳“׳§׳¡ ׳׳—׳™׳₪׳•׳© ׳׳”׳™׳¨ ׳׳₪׳™ ׳“׳׳™׳•׳ ׳•׳§׳˜׳•׳¨׳™
            # ivfflat = ׳׳™׳ ׳“׳§׳¡ ׳׳”׳™׳¨, ׳׳×׳׳™׳ ׳׳¢׳“ ׳›׳׳” ׳׳׳₪׳™ ׳¨׳©׳•׳׳•׳×
            await conn.execute("""
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS contacts_embedding_idx
                ON contacts USING ivfflat (embedding vector_cosine_ops)
                WITH (lists = 10)
            """)

        logger.info("Database initialized: contacts table ready.")

    async def find_match(
        self,
        embedding: np.ndarray,
        threshold: float
    ) -> Optional[Contact]:
        """
        ׳׳—׳₪׳© ׳׳× ׳׳™׳© ׳”׳§׳©׳¨ ׳”׳›׳™ ׳“׳•׳׳” ׳-embedding.
        ׳׳©׳×׳׳© ׳‘-cosine similarity ׳©׳ pgvector.

        <=> = cosine distance (1 - similarity)
        ׳׳›׳: distance ׳§׳˜׳ = ׳“׳׳™׳•׳ ׳’׳‘׳•׳”
        threshold 0.5 similarity = distance 0.5
        """
        # ׳”׳׳¨׳× numpy array ׳׳₪׳•׳¨׳׳˜ ׳©pgvector ׳׳§׳‘׳: "[0.1, 0.2, ...]"
        embedding_str = "[" + ",".join(map(str, embedding.tolist())) + "]"

        async with self._pool.acquire() as conn:
            row = await conn.fetchrow("""
                SELECT id, name, photo_url, enrolled_at,
                       1 - (embedding <=> $1::vector) AS similarity
                FROM contacts
                ORDER BY embedding <=> $1::vector
                LIMIT 1
            """, embedding_str)

        if row is None:
            return None  # ׳׳™׳ ׳׳ ׳©׳™ ׳§׳©׳¨ ׳‘׳›׳׳ ׳‘-DB

        similarity = float(row["similarity"])
        logger.debug("Best match: '%s' with similarity %.3f", row["name"], similarity)

        if similarity < threshold:
            return None  # ׳”׳›׳™ ׳“׳•׳׳”, ׳׳‘׳ ׳¢׳“׳™׳™׳ ׳׳×׳—׳× ׳׳¡׳£

        return Contact(
            id=row["id"],
            name=row["name"],
            photo_url=row["photo_url"],
            enrolled_at=row["enrolled_at"]
        )

    async def save_contact(
        self,
        name: str,
        embedding: np.ndarray,
        photo_url: str
    ) -> Contact:
        """
        ׳©׳•׳׳¨ ׳׳™׳© ׳§׳©׳¨ ׳—׳“׳© ׳•׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ ׳׳•׳×׳• ׳¢׳ ׳”-ID ׳©׳ ׳•׳¦׳¨.
        """
        embedding_str = "[" + ",".join(map(str, embedding.tolist())) + "]"
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        async with self._pool.acquire() as conn:
            row = await conn.fetchrow("""
                INSERT INTO contacts (name, embedding, photo_url, enrolled_at)
                VALUES ($1, $2::vector, $3, $4)
                RETURNING id, name, photo_url, enrolled_at
            """, name, embedding_str, photo_url, now)

        logger.info("Saved new contact: '%s' (id=%s)", row["name"], row["id"])

        return Contact(
            id=row["id"],
            name=row["name"],
            photo_url=row["photo_url"],
            enrolled_at=row["enrolled_at"]
        )

    async def get_all_contacts(self) -> list[Contact]:
        """
        ׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ ׳׳× ׳›׳ ׳׳ ׳©׳™ ׳”׳§׳©׳¨ ג€” ׳‘׳׳™ embeddings.
        """
        async with self._pool.acquire() as conn:
            rows = await conn.fetch("""
                SELECT id, name, photo_url, enrolled_at
                FROM contacts
                ORDER BY enrolled_at DESC
            """)

        return [
            Contact(
                id=row["id"],
                name=row["name"],
                photo_url=row["photo_url"],
                enrolled_at=row["enrolled_at"]
            )
            for row in rows
        ]

    async def get_all_embeddings(self) -> list[tuple[UUID, np.ndarray]]:
        """
        ׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ ׳›׳ ׳”-embeddings ׳׳”-DB ג€” ׳׳©׳™׳׳•׳© ׳‘-cache.
        """
        async with self._pool.acquire() as conn:
            rows = await conn.fetch("SELECT id, embedding FROM contacts")

        result = []
        for row in rows:
            # pgvector ׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ ׳׳× ׳”-embedding ׳›-string "[0.1, 0.2, ...]"
            # ׳׳׳™׳¨ ׳—׳–׳¨׳” ׳-numpy array
            emb_str = row["embedding"]
            values = [float(x) for x in emb_str.strip("[]").split(",")]
            result.append((row["id"], np.array(values, dtype=np.float32)))

        return result

    async def delete_contact(self, contact_id: UUID) -> bool:
        """
        ׳׳•׳—׳§ ׳׳™׳© ׳§׳©׳¨ ׳׳₪׳™ ID.
        ׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ True ׳׳ ׳ ׳׳—׳§, False ׳׳ ׳׳ ׳ ׳׳¦׳.
        """
        async with self._pool.acquire() as conn:
            result = await conn.execute(
                "DELETE FROM contacts WHERE id = $1", contact_id
            )

        # result = "DELETE 1" ׳׳ ׳ ׳׳—׳§, "DELETE 0" ׳׳ ׳׳ ׳ ׳׳¦׳
        deleted = result == "DELETE 1"
        if deleted:
            logger.info("Deleted contact id=%s", contact_id)
        return deleted

# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
# infrastructure/redis_cache.py
#
# ׳׳˜׳¨׳”: cache ׳©׳ embeddings ׳‘-Redis ׳׳‘׳™׳¦׳•׳¢׳™׳ ׳˜׳•׳‘׳™׳ ׳™׳•׳×׳¨.
#
# ׳”׳‘׳¢׳™׳” ׳‘׳׳™ cache:
#   ׳›׳ ׳‘׳§׳©׳× /recognize = ׳©׳׳™׳׳×׳” ׳-DB = I/O = ׳׳™׳˜׳™
#
# ׳”׳₪׳×׳¨׳•׳ ׳¢׳ cache:
#   ׳˜׳¢׳™׳ ׳× ׳›׳ ׳”-embeddings ׳₪׳¢׳ ׳׳—׳× ׳-Redis (TTL: 5 ׳“׳§׳•׳×)
#   ׳›׳ ׳‘׳§׳©׳× /recognize = ׳§׳¨׳™׳׳” ׳-Redis = ׳׳”׳™׳¨
#   ׳׳—׳¨׳™ enroll/delete: cache ׳ ׳׳—׳§ ג†’ ׳ ׳˜׳¢׳ ׳׳—׳“׳© ׳‘׳‘׳§׳©׳” ׳”׳‘׳׳”
#
# ׳₪׳•׳¨׳׳˜ ׳׳—׳¡׳•׳ ׳‘-Redis:
#   key: "face_embeddings"
#   value: JSON array ׳©׳ {id: "uuid", embedding: [0.1, 0.2, ...]}
# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€

import redis.asyncio as aioredis
import numpy as np
import json
from uuid import UUID
from typing import Optional
import logging

from app.core.interfaces import IEmbeddingCache
from app.config import settings

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# ׳›׳׳” ׳–׳׳ ׳”-cache ׳×׳§׳£ (׳©׳ ׳™׳•׳×)
CACHE_TTL_SECONDS = 300  # 5 ׳“׳§׳•׳×
CACHE_KEY = "face_embeddings"


class RedisEmbeddingCache(IEmbeddingCache):
    """
    Cache ׳©׳ embeddings ׳‘-Redis.
    ׳׳׳™׳¥ ׳׳× ׳—׳™׳₪׳•׳© ׳”׳“׳׳™׳•׳ ׳‘׳׳™ ׳׳˜׳¢׳•׳ ׳׳”-DB ׳‘׳›׳ ׳‘׳§׳©׳”.
    """

    def __init__(self, redis_client: aioredis.Redis):
        self._redis = redis_client

    async def get_all_embeddings(self) -> Optional[list[tuple[UUID, np.ndarray]]]:
        """
        ׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ embeddings ׳׳”-cache.
        None = cache ׳¨׳™׳§ (׳¦׳¨׳™׳ ׳׳˜׳¢׳•׳ ׳׳”-DB).
        """
        try:
            data = await self._redis.get(CACHE_KEY)
            if data is None:
                return None  # cache miss

            # ׳₪׳¢׳ ׳•׳— JSON ג†’ ׳¨׳©׳™׳׳× (UUID, numpy array)
            items = json.loads(data)
            result = []
            for item in items:
                contact_id = UUID(item["id"])
                embedding = np.array(item["embedding"], dtype=np.float32)
                result.append((contact_id, embedding))

            logger.debug("Cache hit: loaded %d embeddings from Redis", len(result))
            return result

        except Exception as e:
            # ׳׳ Redis ׳׳ ׳–׳׳™׳ ג€” ׳׳׳©׳™׳ ׳‘׳׳™ cache (degraded mode)
            logger.warning("Redis cache read failed: %s", e)
            return None

    async def set_all_embeddings(
        self,
        embeddings: list[tuple[UUID, np.ndarray]]
    ) -> None:
        """
        ׳©׳•׳׳¨ embeddings ׳‘-cache ׳¢׳ TTL ׳©׳ 5 ׳“׳§׳•׳×.
        """
        try:
            # ׳”׳׳¨׳” ׳-JSON-serializable format
            items = [
                {
                    "id": str(contact_id),
                    "embedding": embedding.tolist()  # numpy ג†’ list ׳¨׳’׳™׳
                }
                for contact_id, embedding in embeddings
            ]

            await self._redis.setex(
                CACHE_KEY,
                CACHE_TTL_SECONDS,
                json.dumps(items)
            )

            logger.debug("Cached %d embeddings in Redis (TTL=%ds)", len(items), CACHE_TTL_SECONDS)

        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("Redis cache write failed: %s", e)

    async def invalidate(self) -> None:
        """
        ׳׳ ׳§׳” ׳׳× ׳”-cache ג€” ׳ ׳§׳¨׳ ׳׳—׳¨׳™ enroll ׳׳• delete.
        ׳”׳‘׳§׳©׳” ׳”׳‘׳׳” ׳×׳˜׳¢׳•׳ ׳׳—׳“׳© ׳׳”-DB.
        """
        try:
            await self._redis.delete(CACHE_KEY)
            logger.debug("Embedding cache invalidated")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("Redis cache invalidate failed: %s", e)

# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
# main.py
#
# ׳׳˜׳¨׳”: ׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳× ׳”׳›׳ ׳™׳¡׳” ׳©׳ ׳”׳©׳™׳¨׳•׳× ג€” ׳׳’׳“׳™׳¨ ׳׳× FastAPI app,
# ׳׳—׳‘׳¨ ׳׳× ׳›׳ ׳”-endpoints, ׳•׳׳ ׳”׳ ׳׳× ׳׳—׳–׳•׳¨ ׳”׳—׳™׳™׳.
#
# Lifespan (startup/shutdown):
#   startup  ג†’ ׳—׳™׳‘׳•׳¨ ׳-DB ׳•-Redis, ׳˜׳¢׳™׳ ׳× ׳׳•׳“׳ InsightFace, ׳™׳¦׳™׳¨׳× ׳˜׳‘׳׳׳•׳×
#   shutdown ג†’ ׳¡׳’׳™׳¨׳× ׳—׳™׳‘׳•׳¨׳™׳ ׳‘׳¦׳•׳¨׳” ׳׳¡׳•׳“׳¨׳×
#
# Static files:
#   /photos ג†’ ׳׳’׳™׳© ׳׳× ׳×׳׳•׳ ׳•׳× ׳”׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ׳™׳©׳™׳¨׳•׳× (׳‘׳׳™ ׳¦׳•׳¨׳ ׳‘-nginx)
#   Frontend ׳™׳’׳© ׳: http://localhost:8001/photos/uuid.jpg
# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€

import asyncio
import logging
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

import asyncpg
import redis.asyncio as aioredis
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles

from app.config import settings
from app.infrastructure.insightface_detector import InsightFaceDetector
from app.infrastructure.postgres_store import PostgresContactStore
from app.infrastructure.redis_cache import RedisEmbeddingCache
from app.api import recognize, enroll, contacts

# ׳”׳’׳“׳¨׳× logging ג€” ׳₪׳•׳¨׳׳˜ ׳©׳׳¨׳׳” ׳–׳׳, ׳¨׳׳”, ׳•׳׳§׳•׳¨
logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s | %(levelname)s | %(name)s | %(message)s"
)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    """
    ׳׳ ׳”׳ ׳׳× ׳׳—׳–׳•׳¨ ׳”׳—׳™׳™׳ ׳©׳ ׳”׳׳₪׳׳™׳§׳¦׳™׳”.
    ׳§׳•׳“ ׳׳₪׳ ׳™ yield = startup, ׳§׳•׳“ ׳׳—׳¨׳™ yield = shutdown.
    """
    # ג”€ג”€ STARTUP ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
    logger.info("Starting GazeConnect Face Recognition Service...")

    # 1. ׳—׳™׳‘׳•׳¨ ׳-PostgreSQL (pool ׳©׳ 5-20 ׳—׳™׳‘׳•׳¨׳™׳)
    logger.info("Connecting to PostgreSQL...")
    pool = await asyncpg.create_pool(
    dsn=settings.database_url.replace("+asyncpg", ""),
    min_size=5,
    max_size=20,
    ssl=False
)

    # 2. ׳—׳™׳‘׳•׳¨ ׳-Redis
    logger.info("Connecting to Redis...")
    redis_client = aioredis.from_url(
        settings.redis_url,
        encoding="utf-8",
        decode_responses=True
    )

    # 3. ׳™׳¦׳™׳¨׳× infrastructure objects
    contact_store = PostgresContactStore(pool)
    cache = RedisEmbeddingCache(redis_client)

    # 4. ׳™׳¦׳™׳¨׳× ׳˜׳‘׳׳× contacts (׳׳ ׳׳ ׳§׳™׳™׳׳×)
    await contact_store.initialize()

    # 5. ׳˜׳¢׳™׳ ׳× ׳׳•׳“׳ InsightFace (׳”׳›׳‘׳“ ׳‘׳™׳•׳×׳¨ ג€” ~200MB, ~30 ׳©׳ ׳™׳•׳×)
    logger.info("Loading InsightFace model (this may take ~30 seconds)...")
    detector = InsightFaceDetector()

    # 6. ׳©׳׳™׳¨׳× ׳”׳›׳ ׳‘-app state (׳׳©׳•׳×׳£ ׳׳›׳ ׳”׳‘׳§׳©׳•׳×)
    app.state.pool = pool
    app.state.redis = redis_client
    app.state.contact_store = contact_store
    app.state.cache = cache
    app.state.detector = detector

    logger.info("ג… Face Recognition Service is ready!")

    yield  # ג† ׳›׳׳ ׳”׳©׳™׳¨׳•׳× ׳¨׳¥ ׳•׳׳§׳‘׳ ׳‘׳§׳©׳•׳×

    # ג”€ג”€ SHUTDOWN ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
    logger.info("Shutting down...")
    await pool.close()
    await redis_client.close()
    logger.info("Connections closed. Goodbye!")


# ג”€ג”€ FastAPI App ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
app = FastAPI(
    title="GazeConnect Face Recognition Service",

# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
# services/enrollment_service.py
#
# ׳׳˜׳¨׳”: ׳׳•׳’׳™׳§׳× ׳”׳¨׳©׳׳× ׳׳“׳ ׳—׳“׳© ׳׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ("enroll").
#
# ׳–׳¨׳™׳׳× ׳”׳¨׳©׳׳”:
#   1. ׳׳§׳‘׳ ׳×׳׳•׳ ׳” + ׳©׳ ׳׳”-Admin
#   2. InsightFace ׳׳•׳¦׳ ׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ׳‘׳×׳׳•׳ ׳” + ׳׳—׳©׳‘ embedding
#   3. ׳—׳™׳×׳•׳ ׳”׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ׳•׳©׳׳™׳¨׳× ׳×׳׳•׳ ׳” ׳§׳˜׳ ׳” ׳׳“׳™׳¡׳§
#   4. ׳©׳׳™׳¨׳× (׳©׳, embedding, photo_url) ׳‘-DB
#   5. ׳׳—׳™׳§׳× cache ׳›׳“׳™ ׳©׳”׳–׳™׳”׳•׳™ ׳™׳›׳™׳¨ ׳׳× ׳”׳׳“׳ ׳”׳—׳“׳© ׳׳™׳“
#
# ׳׳” ׳ ׳©׳׳¨:
#   /app/photos/{uuid}.jpg  ג† ׳×׳׳•׳ ׳× ׳”׳₪׳ ׳™׳ (Docker volume)
#   contacts ׳˜׳‘׳׳” ג† embedding + metadata
# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€

import cv2
import numpy as np
import os
from uuid import uuid4
import logging

from app.core.interfaces import IFaceDetector, IContactStore, IEmbeddingCache
from app.core.models import Contact, EnrollResponse
from app.config import settings

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class EnrollmentService:
    """
    ׳¨׳™׳©׳•׳ ׳׳ ׳©׳™ ׳§׳©׳¨ ׳—׳“׳©׳™׳ ׳׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳×.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        detector: IFaceDetector,
        store: IContactStore,
        cache: IEmbeddingCache
    ):
        self._detector = detector
        self._store = store
        self._cache = cache

        # ׳•׳™׳“׳•׳ ׳©׳×׳™׳§׳™׳™׳× ׳”׳×׳׳•׳ ׳•׳× ׳§׳™׳™׳׳×
        os.makedirs(settings.photos_dir, exist_ok=True)

    async def enroll(self, name: str, image_bytes: bytes) -> EnrollResponse:
        """
        ׳¨׳•׳©׳ ׳׳“׳ ׳—׳“׳© ׳׳×׳׳•׳ ׳”.
        ׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ ׳₪׳¨׳˜׳™ ׳”׳”׳¨׳©׳׳” ׳›׳•׳׳ ׳”-photo_url.
        """
        # ׳©׳׳‘ 1: ׳–׳™׳”׳•׳™ ׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ׳‘׳×׳׳•׳ ׳”
        detected = await self._detector.detect_and_embed(image_bytes)

        if not detected:
            raise ValueError("No face detected in the provided image")

        if len(detected) > 1:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Multiple faces detected ({len(detected)}). "
                "Please provide an image with exactly one person."
            )

        embedding, bbox = detected[0]

        # ׳©׳׳‘ 2: ׳©׳׳™׳¨׳× ׳×׳׳•׳ ׳× ׳”׳₪׳ ׳™׳
        photo_url = await self._save_face_photo(image_bytes, bbox)

        # ׳©׳׳‘ 3: ׳©׳׳™׳¨׳” ׳‘-DB
        contact = await self._store.save_contact(
            name=name,
            embedding=embedding,
            photo_url=photo_url
        )

        # ׳©׳׳‘ 4: ׳׳—׳™׳§׳× cache ג€” ׳›׳“׳™ ׳©׳”׳׳“׳ ׳”׳—׳“׳© ׳™׳–׳•׳”׳” ׳׳™׳“
        await self._cache.invalidate()

        logger.info("Enrolled '%s' successfully (id=%s)", name, contact.id)

        return EnrollResponse(
            contact_id=contact.id,
            name=contact.name,
            photo_url=photo_url,
            message=f"'{name}' enrolled successfully"
        )

    async def _save_face_photo(
        self,
        image_bytes: bytes,
        bbox: tuple[int, int, int, int]
    ) -> str:
        """
        ׳—׳•׳×׳ ׳׳× ׳”׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ׳׳”׳×׳׳•׳ ׳” ׳•׳©׳•׳׳¨ ׳›׳§׳•׳‘׳¥ JPEG.
        ׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ ׳׳× ׳”-URL ׳”׳™׳—׳¡׳™: "/photos/uuid.jpg"

        ׳׳׳” ׳׳—׳×׳•׳? ׳›׳™ ׳׳ ׳—׳ ׳• ׳©׳•׳׳¨׳™׳ ׳¨׳§ ׳׳× ׳”׳₪׳ ׳™׳ (׳׳ ׳›׳ ׳”׳×׳׳•׳ ׳”):
        - ׳’׳•׳“׳ ׳§׳˜׳ ׳™׳•׳×׳¨
        - ׳₪׳¨׳˜׳™׳•׳× ׳˜׳•׳‘׳” ׳™׳•׳×׳¨
        - ׳×׳¦׳•׳’׳” ׳˜׳•׳‘׳” ׳™׳•׳×׳¨ ׳¢׳ ׳›׳₪׳×׳•׳¨ AAC
        """
        # ׳₪׳¢׳ ׳•׳— ׳”׳×׳׳•׳ ׳”
        nparr = np.frombuffer(image_bytes, np.uint8)
        img = cv2.imdecode(nparr, cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)

        x, y, w, h = bbox

        # ׳”׳•׳¡׳₪׳× padding ׳©׳ 20% ׳¡׳‘׳™׳‘ ׳”׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ׳׳×׳׳•׳ ׳” ׳ ׳¢׳™׳׳” ׳™׳•׳×׳¨
        padding_x = int(w * 0.2)
        padding_y = int(h * 0.2)

        # ׳•׳™׳“׳•׳ ׳©׳׳ ׳™׳•׳¦׳׳™׳ ׳׳’׳‘׳•׳׳•׳× ׳”׳×׳׳•׳ ׳”
        img_h, img_w = img.shape[:2]
        x1 = max(0, x - padding_x)
        y1 = max(0, y - padding_y)
        x2 = min(img_w, x + w + padding_x)
        y2 = min(img_h, y + h + padding_y)

        # ׳—׳™׳×׳•׳ ׳”׳₪׳ ׳™׳
        face_crop = img[y1:y2, x1:x2]

        # ׳©׳׳™׳¨׳” ׳›-JPEG
        photo_filename = f"{uuid4()}.jpg"
        photo_path = os.path.join(settings.photos_dir, photo_filename)
        cv2.imwrite(photo_path, face_crop, [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, 90])

        logger.debug("Saved face photo: %s", photo_path)

        # ׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ URL ׳™׳—׳¡׳™ ג€” ׳”-Frontend ׳™׳‘׳ ׳”: http://localhost:8001/photos/uuid.jpg
        return f"/photos/{photo_filename}"

FILE: E:\B\Project\GazeConnect\services\python\face_recognition\app\services\recognition_service.py

# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€
# services/recognition_service.py
#
# ׳׳˜׳¨׳”: ׳׳•׳’׳™׳§׳× ׳”׳–׳™׳”׳•׳™ ׳”׳׳¨׳›׳–׳™׳× ג€” ׳׳—׳‘׳¨ ׳‘׳™׳ InsightFace, DB, ׳•-Cache.
#
# ׳–׳¨׳™׳׳× ׳–׳™׳”׳•׳™ ׳׳›׳ ׳‘׳§׳©׳”:
#   1. InsightFace ג†’ ׳׳•׳¦׳ ׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ׳‘׳×׳׳•׳ ׳” + ׳׳—׳©׳‘ embeddings
#   2. Redis cache ג†’ ׳”׳׳ ׳™׳© embeddings ׳©׳׳•׳¨׳™׳?
#      - ׳›׳: ׳—׳™׳©׳•׳‘ cosine similarity ׳׳•׳ cache
#      - ׳׳: ׳˜׳¢׳™׳ ׳” ׳׳”-DB ג†’ ׳©׳׳™׳¨׳” ׳‘-cache ג†’ ׳—׳™׳©׳•׳‘
#   3. ׳”׳©׳•׳•׳׳”: ׳›׳ ׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ׳׳•׳ ׳›׳ ׳׳™׳© ׳§׳©׳¨ ג†’ ׳׳™ ׳”׳›׳™ ׳“׳•׳׳”?
#   4. ׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ ׳×׳•׳¦׳׳•׳× ׳‘׳₪׳•׳¨׳׳˜ ׳©C# ׳׳¦׳₪׳” ׳׳§׳‘׳
# ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€ג”€

import numpy as np
import logging
from uuid import UUID

from app.core.interfaces import IFaceDetector, IContactStore, IEmbeddingCache
from app.core.models import FaceDetectionResponse, BoundingBox
from app.config import settings

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class RecognitionService:
    """
    ׳׳•׳’׳™׳§׳× ׳–׳™׳”׳•׳™ ׳₪׳ ׳™׳: ׳׳×׳׳•׳ ׳” ׳’׳•׳׳׳™׳× ג†’ ׳×׳•׳¦׳׳•׳× ׳׳™ ׳ ׳׳¦׳ ׳‘׳×׳׳•׳ ׳”.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        detector: IFaceDetector,
        store: IContactStore,
        cache: IEmbeddingCache
    ):
        # Dependency Injection ג€” ׳׳§׳‘׳ ׳׳× ׳›׳ ׳”׳×׳׳•׳™׳•׳× ׳׳‘׳—׳•׳¥
        # ׳–׳” ׳׳׳₪׳©׳¨ ׳׳”׳—׳׳™׳£ ׳›׳ ׳—׳׳§ ׳‘׳ ׳₪׳¨׳“ (׳׳׳©׳ ׳‘-tests: MockDetector)
        self._detector = detector
        self._store = store
        self._cache = cache

    async def recognize(self, image_bytes: bytes) -> list[FaceDetectionResponse]:
        """
        ׳”׳₪׳•׳ ׳§׳¦׳™׳” ׳”׳¨׳׳©׳™׳× ג€” ׳׳§׳‘׳ ׳×׳׳•׳ ׳”, ׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ ׳׳™ ׳ ׳׳¦׳ ׳‘׳”.
        """
        # ׳©׳׳‘ 1: ׳–׳™׳”׳•׳™ ׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ׳‘׳×׳׳•׳ ׳”
        detected_faces = await self._detector.detect_and_embed(image_bytes)

        if not detected_faces:
            return []  # ׳׳™׳ ׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ׳‘׳×׳׳•׳ ׳”

        # ׳©׳׳‘ 2: ׳˜׳¢׳™׳ ׳× embeddings ׳׳•׳›׳¨׳™׳ (׳-cache ׳׳• DB)
        known_embeddings = await self._load_known_embeddings()

        # ׳©׳׳‘ 3: ׳”׳©׳•׳•׳׳× ׳›׳ ׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ׳©׳–׳•׳”׳• ׳׳•׳ ׳›׳ ׳׳™׳© ׳§׳©׳¨ ׳׳•׳›׳¨
        results = []
        for face_embedding, bbox in detected_faces:
            result = await self._match_face(face_embedding, bbox, known_embeddings)
            results.append(result)

        return results

    async def _load_known_embeddings(self) -> list[tuple[UUID, np.ndarray]]:
        """
        ׳˜׳•׳¢׳ embeddings ׳-Redis cache.
        ׳׳ ׳”-cache ׳¨׳™׳§ ג€” ׳˜׳•׳¢׳ ׳׳”-DB ׳•׳©׳•׳׳¨ ׳‘-cache.
        """
        # ׳ ׳™׳¡׳™׳•׳ ׳׳˜׳¢׳•׳ ׳-cache ׳§׳•׳“׳
        cached = await self._cache.get_all_embeddings()
        if cached is not None:
            return cached  # cache hit ג€” ׳׳”׳™׳¨!

        # cache miss ג€” ׳˜׳•׳¢׳ ׳™׳ ׳׳”-DB
        logger.debug("Cache miss ג€” loading embeddings from database")
        db_embeddings = await self._store.get_all_embeddings()

        # ׳©׳•׳׳¨׳™׳ ׳‘-cache ׳׳‘׳§׳©׳•׳× ׳”׳‘׳׳•׳×
        if db_embeddings:
            await self._cache.set_all_embeddings(db_embeddings)

        return db_embeddings

    async def _match_face(
        self,
        face_embedding: np.ndarray,
        bbox: tuple[int, int, int, int],
        known_embeddings: list[tuple[UUID, np.ndarray]]
    ) -> FaceDetectionResponse:
        """
        ׳׳©׳•׳•׳” ׳₪׳ ׳™׳ ׳׳—׳“ ׳׳•׳ ׳›׳ ׳׳ ׳©׳™ ׳”׳§׳©׳¨ ׳”׳׳•׳›׳¨׳™׳.
        ׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ FaceDetectionResponse ׳¢׳ ׳₪׳¨׳˜׳™ ׳”׳–׳™׳”׳•׳™.
        """
        x, y, w, h = bbox
        bounding_box = BoundingBox(x=x, y=y, width=w, height=h)

        if not known_embeddings:
            # ׳׳™׳ ׳׳ ׳©׳™ ׳§׳©׳¨ ׳¨׳©׳•׳׳™׳ ׳›׳׳
            return FaceDetectionResponse(
                person_id=None,
                person_name=None,
                confidence=0.0,
                bounding_box=bounding_box,
                is_known=False
            )

        # ׳—׳™׳©׳•׳‘ cosine similarity ׳׳•׳ ׳›׳ ׳׳™׳© ׳§׳©׳¨
        best_id, best_similarity = self._find_best_match(face_embedding, known_embeddings)

        if best_similarity >= settings.recognition_threshold:
            # ׳׳“׳ ׳׳•׳›׳¨! ג€” ׳¦׳¨׳™׳ ׳׳©׳׳•׳£ ׳׳× ׳©׳׳• ׳׳”-DB
            contact = await self._store.find_match(face_embedding, settings.recognition_threshold)

            if contact:
                return FaceDetectionResponse(
                    person_id=contact.id,
                    person_name=contact.name,
                    confidence=best_similarity,
                    bounding_box=bounding_box,
                    is_known=True
                )

        # ׳׳“׳ ׳׳ ׳׳•׳›׳¨
        return FaceDetectionResponse(
            person_id=None,
            person_name=None,
            confidence=best_similarity,
            bounding_box=bounding_box,
            is_known=False
        )

    def _find_best_match(
        self,
        query_embedding: np.ndarray,
        known_embeddings: list[tuple[UUID, np.ndarray]]
    ) -> tuple[UUID, float]:
        """
        ׳—׳™׳©׳•׳‘ cosine similarity ׳‘׳™׳ query ׳׳›׳ ׳”-embeddings ׳”׳׳•׳›׳¨׳™׳.
        ׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ (UUID, similarity) ׳©׳ ׳”׳”׳×׳׳׳” ׳”׳˜׳•׳‘׳” ׳‘׳™׳•׳×׳¨.

        cosine similarity = (A ֲ· B) / (|A| ֳ— |B|)
        ׳×׳•׳¦׳׳” ׳‘׳™׳ 0 (׳©׳•׳ ׳” ׳׳—׳׳•׳˜׳™׳) ׳-1 (׳–׳”׳”)
        """
        # ׳ ׳¨׳׳•׳ ׳”-query embedding ׳׳׳•׳¨׳ 1 (׳™׳¢׳™׳ ׳—׳™׳©׳•׳‘׳™׳×)
        query_norm = query_embedding / (np.linalg.norm(query_embedding) + 1e-8)

        best_id = None
        best_similarity = -1.0

        for contact_id, known_embedding in known_embeddings:
            # ׳ ׳¨׳׳•׳ ׳”-known embedding
            known_norm = known_embedding / (np.linalg.norm(known_embedding) + 1e-8)

            # ׳׳›׳₪׳׳” ׳₪׳ ׳™׳׳™׳× ׳©׳ ׳•׳§׳˜׳•׳¨׳™׳ ׳׳ ׳•׳¨׳׳׳™׳ = cosine similarity
            similarity = float(np.dot(query_norm, known_norm))

            if similarity > best_similarity:
                best_similarity = similarity
                best_id = contact_id

        return best_id, best_similarity
